            function getRedirectTo(domain, locale, hasParams) {
                if (hasParams) {
                    return window.location.origin.replace(window.location.origin.substring(window.location.origin.lastIndexOf('.') + 1), domain) + "/" + locale + window.location.search;
                } else {

                }

<script type="text/javascript">
	var cnRedirectTo = ""
	var cnPathLocation = str2.substring(str2.indexOf('/'));

	function getChinacnRedirectTo(domain, redirectLocale) {
		return window.location.origin.replace(window.location.origin.substring(window.location.origin.lastIndexOf('.') + 1), domain) + "/" + redirectLocale + cnPathLocation + window.location.search;
	}

	if (isChinaIP) {
		if (locale == "en-cn") {
			if (!isEnableEnCnRedirect) {
				//scenario#4
				cnRedirectTo = getChinacnRedirectTo("cn", "en-cn"); // vertiv.cn/en-cn
			}
		} else if (locale == "zh-cn") {
			//scenario#5
			cnRedirectTo = getChinacnRedirectTo("cn", "zh-cn"); // vertiv.cn/zh-cn
		}
	} else {
		//Outsite CN
		if (locale == "en-cn") {
			if (!isEnableEnCnRedirect) {
				//scenario#13 and scenario#13.5
				cnRedirectTo = getChinacnRedirectTo("cn", "en-cn");; // vertiv.cn/en-cn
			}
		} else if (locale == "zh-cn") {
			//scenario#14
			cnRedirectTo = getChinacnRedirectTo("cn", "zh-cn"); // vertiv.cn/zh-cn
		}
	}
	if (locale == "episerver") {
		cnRedirectTo = "";
	}
	if (window.location.hostname == "prod.vertiv.cn") {
		if (locale == "zh-cn") {
			cnRedirectTo = "https://www.vertiv.cn" + "/" + "zh-cn" + cnPathLocation + window.location.search;
		}
	}
	if (cnRedirectTo != "") {
		window.location = cnRedirectTo;
		//alert("redirectTo:" + redirectTo);
	}
</script>

<script>
	$(document).ready(function () {

		function removeExtension() {
			$(".container .tabs-left-component ul li a:contains('.pdf')").each(function () {
				var currentText = $(this).text();
				var newText = currentText.replace('.pdf', '');
				$(this).text(newText);
			});
		}
		removeExtension();
		$(".container .tabs-left-component ul li").click(function () {
			removeExtension();
		});
	});
</script>

<script>

(function () {
  function cleanInsightsMenu() {
    if (!window.matchMedia("(max-width: 767px)").matches) return;

    const insights = [...document.querySelectorAll('.mobile-nav__item')]
      .find(el =>
        el.querySelector('.mobile-nav__item_title_text')?.textContent.trim() === 'INSIGHTS'
      );

    if (!insights) return;

    const submenu = insights.querySelector('.mobile-nav__submenu');
    if (!submenu) return;

    if (submenu.dataset.cleaned === "true") return;

    const links = [...submenu.querySelectorAll('a.mobile-nav__submenu_link')];

    let overview = null;
    const topics = [];

    links.forEach(link => {
      const text = link.textContent.trim().toLowerCase();

      if (text === 'overview' && !overview) {
        overview = link.cloneNode(true);
      } else if (text !== 'overview') {
        topics.push(link.cloneNode(true));
      }
    });

    submenu.innerHTML = '';

    if (overview) submenu.appendChild(overview);
    topics.forEach(link => submenu.appendChild(link));


    submenu.dataset.cleaned = "true";
  }


  setInterval(cleanInsightsMenu, 800);

  cleanInsightsMenu();
})();

</script>

<script>
// 09022026 PST-424 DAVID ADDED WORKAROUND
// Nav workarounds: Industries has no landing page
(function () {
    if (!window.jQuery) return;
    jQuery(document).ready(function ($) {
        // Define industry page paths, no locale prefix (add more as needed)
        var industryPages = [
            '/solutions/colocation-data-centers/',
            '/solutions/education/',
            '/solutions/finance/',
            '/solutions/healthcare/',
            '/solutions/hyperscale-and-cloud/',
            '/solutions/manufacturing/',
            '/solutions/logistics/',
            '/solutions/retail/',
            '/solutions/government/',
            '/solutions/industries/telecom/'
        ];
        // Split a path/href into segments, e.g. '/it-emea/industries/' -> ['it-emea','industries']
        function segments(href) {
            href = (href || '').toLowerCase()
                .replace(/^https?:\/\/[^\/]+/, '')
                .split('?')[0].split('#')[0];
            return href.split('/').filter(Boolean);
        }
        // True if the href is an Industries landing page: /<locale>/industries/
        function isIndustriesHref(href) {
            var parts = segments(href);
            return parts.length === 2 && parts[1] === 'industries';
        }
        // ---- 1. Disable Industries nav click (no landing page available) ----
        $('a.desktop-nav__link').each(function () {
            if (isIndustriesHref($(this).attr('href'))) {
                $(this).removeAttr('href').css({
                    'cursor': 'default',
                    'text-decoration': 'none'
                });
            }
        });
        // ---- 2. Hide Industries "Overview" link in mobile nav ----
        // Matched by href only, so translated labels (Presentazione, etc.) are covered
        $('a.mobile-nav__submenu_link').each(function () {
            if (isIndustriesHref($(this).attr('href'))) {
                $(this).removeAttr('href').hide();
            }
        });
        // ---- 3. Move active state from Solutions to Industries on industry pages ----
        // Drop the locale segment, then compare against industryPages
        var parts = segments(window.location.pathname);
        if (parts.length < 2) return;
        var pathNoLocale = '/' + parts.slice(1).join('/') + '/';
        if (industryPages.indexOf(pathNoLocale) === -1) return;
        // Solutions: matched by href (URL slug is not translated)
        $('.desktop-nav__item').filter(function () {
            var href = ($(this).find('.desktop-nav__link').first().attr('href') || '').toLowerCase();
            return href.indexOf('/solutions/') !== -1;
        }).removeClass('is-active');
        // Industries: the only nav item whose link has no href
        // (stripped in step 1 above, or already hrefless in the CMS)
        $('.desktop-nav__item').filter(function () {
            var $link = $(this).find('.desktop-nav__link').first();
            return $link.length && !$link.attr('href');
        }).first().addClass('is-active');
    });
})();
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
// 08292026 PST-421 DAVID ADDED WORKAROUND
    (function () {

        var hiddenTopics = [
            'News release | Partnerships',
            'News release | Artificial Intelligence (AI)',
            'News release | Enterprise',
            'News release | Edge Computing',
            'News release | Cloud, Colo, Hyperscale',
            'News release | Efficiency & Optimization',
            'News release | Industry Trends',
            'News release | Acquisition',
            'News release | Company Updates',
            'News release | Industry Events',
            'News release | Products and Services',
            'Spotlight'
        ];

        function normalize(text) {
            return (text || '')
                .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
                .replace(/\s*\(\d[\d,\s]*\)\s*$/, '')
                .trim()
                .toLowerCase();
        }
        var hiddenTopicsLower = hiddenTopics.map(normalize);
        function isHidden(text) {
            return hiddenTopicsLower.indexOf(normalize(text)) > -1;
        }
        function hideMatchingTopics() {
            // Pattern 1: p3-checkbox labels — hide the label itself.
            var labels = document.querySelectorAll('label.p3-checkbox');
            for (var i = 0; i < labels.length; i++) {
                var label = labels[i];
                var textEl = label.querySelector('.p3-checkbox__text, .p3-checkbox_text');
                if (!textEl) {
                    continue;
                }
                if (isHidden(textEl.textContent)) {
                    label.style.display = 'none';
                }
            }
            // Pattern 2: checkbox-custom-label facets — hide the wrapping <li>.
            var facetLabels = document.querySelectorAll('label.checkbox-custom-label');
            for (var j = 0; j < facetLabels.length; j++) {
                var facetLabel = facetLabels[j];
                if (!isHidden(facetLabel.textContent)) {
                    continue;
                }
                var li = facetLabel.closest ? facetLabel.closest('li') : null;
                if (li) {
                    li.style.display = 'none';
                } else {
                    facetLabel.style.display = 'none';
                }
            }
        }
        hideMatchingTopics();
        var observer = new MutationObserver(function () {
            hideMatchingTopics();
        });
        
        observer.observe(document.documentElement, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    })();
</script>


<script type="text/javascript">
// 08302026 PST-422 DAVID ADDED WORKAROUND FOR HIDING TOPICS FILTER ON NEWS RELEASES
    (function () {
        var segments = window.location.pathname.toLowerCase().split('/').filter(Boolean);
        var pathWithoutLocale = segments.slice(1).join('/');

        var topicFilterPaths = [
            'about/news-and-events/corporate-news',
            'about/news-and-events/news-releases'
        ];

        if (topicFilterPaths.indexOf(pathWithoutLocale) === -1) {
            return;
        }

        function hideTopicFilter() {
            document.querySelectorAll('.search-about-news-filter-component > div.topic.location-selector').forEach(function (el) {
                el.style.display = 'none';
            });
        }

        function init() {
            hideTopicFilter();
            new MutationObserver(hideTopicFilter).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
        }

        if (document.body) {
            init();
        } else {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
        }
    })();
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    // TEMP WORKAROUND (PST-452): the missing elqTrackPageView
    (function () {
        var originalOptanonWrapper = window.OptanonWrapper;
        if (typeof originalOptanonWrapper !== 'function') return;

        var pageViewTracked = false;

        function isEloquaGroupActive() {
            if (typeof Optanon === 'undefined' || typeof OnetrustActiveGroups === 'undefined') return false;
            var json = Optanon.GetDomainData();
            for (var i = 0; i < json.Groups.length; i += 1) {
                var group = json.Groups[i];
                if (group.Cookies && group.Cookies.length > 0) {
                    for (var k = 0; k < group.Cookies.length; k += 1) {
                        if (group.Cookies[k].Name === 'ELOQUA') {
                            var groupId = group.Parent ? group.Parent.OptanonGroupId : group.OptanonGroupId;
                            return OnetrustActiveGroups.indexOf(',' + groupId + ',') !== -1;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            return false;
        }

        window.OptanonWrapper = function () {
            originalOptanonWrapper.apply(this, arguments);
            try {
                if (!pageViewTracked && isEloquaGroupActive()) {
                    pageViewTracked = true;
                    window._elqQ = window._elqQ || [];
                    window._elqQ.push(['elqTrackPageView', window.location.href]);
                }
            } catch (e) { }
        };
    })();

                        function UrlExists(url) {
                            var http = new XMLHttpRequest();
                            http.open('HEAD', url, false);
                            http.send();
                            if (http.status != 404)
                                return true;
                            else
                                return false;
                        }

    <script type="text/javascript">
        function trackUTMParameters() {
            var hash = window.location.hash || '';
            var currentSearch = window.location.search;
            var urlRef = (document.referrer || '').toLowerCase();
            var utmsInUrl = {};

                    var recoveredFromHash = false;
                    // Handle malformed URLs like:
                    // /page/#/?utm_source=linkedin&utm_medium=social

                    if (!currentSearch && hash.indexOf('?') > -1) {
                        currentSearch = '?' + hash.split('?')[1];
                        recoveredFromHash = true;
                    }

                    if (currentSearch.length > 1) {
                        currentSearch.substring(1).split('&').forEach(function (pair) {
                            var eqIdx = pair.indexOf('=');
                            if (eqIdx > -1) {
                                var key = decodeURIComponent(pair.substring(0, eqIdx)).toLowerCase();
                                var val = decodeURIComponent(pair.substring(eqIdx + 1).replace(/\+/g, ' '));
                                if (key.indexOf('utm_') === 0) {
                                    utmsInUrl[key] = val;
                                }
                            }
                        });
                    }

                    if (Object.keys(utmsInUrl).length > 0) {
                        Object.keys(utmsInUrl).forEach(function (key) {
                            sessionStorage.setItem(key, utmsInUrl[key]);
                        });

                if (recoveredFromHash) {
                    var realHash = hash.split('?')[0];
                    var base = window.location.protocol + '//' + window.location.host + window.location.pathname;
                    var normalizedUrl = base + currentSearch;
                    window.history.replaceState({ path: normalizedUrl }, '', normalizedUrl + realHash);
                }
            } else {
                var stored = [];
                for (var i = 0; i < sessionStorage.length; i++) {
                    var key = sessionStorage.key(i);
                    if (key && key.indexOf('utm_') === 0) {
                        var val = sessionStorage.getItem(key);
                        if (val) stored.push(key + '=' + encodeURIComponent(val));
                    }
                }

                // Brand-new tab (nothing captured here yet): if we were opened
                // (link or window.open) from a page that had UTM params, inherit them.
                if (stored.length === 0 && urlRef.indexOf('utm_') > -1 && urlRef.indexOf('?') > -1) {
                    var refQuery = urlRef.substring(urlRef.indexOf('?') + 1);
                    refQuery.split('&').forEach(function (pair) {
                        var eqIdx = pair.indexOf('=');
                        if (eqIdx > -1) {
                            var key = decodeURIComponent(pair.substring(0, eqIdx)).toLowerCase();
                            if (key.indexOf('utm_') === 0) {
                                var val = decodeURIComponent(pair.substring(eqIdx + 1).replace(/\+/g, ' '));
                                sessionStorage.setItem(key, val);
                                stored.push(key + '=' + encodeURIComponent(val));
                            }
                        }
                    });
                }

                        if (stored.length > 0) {
                            var base = window.location.protocol + '//' + window.location.host + window.location.pathname;
                            var qs = currentSearch.length > 0 ? currentSearch + '&' : '?';
                            var newUrl = base + qs + stored.join('&');
                            window.history.replaceState({ path: newUrl }, '', newUrl + hash);
                        }
                    }
                }

                trackUTMParameters();
                window.addEventListener('hashchange', trackUTMParameters);
    </script>

<script>
    $(window).on('load', function () {
        document.querySelectorAll(".p3-page-footer__submenu > a").forEach(function (element) {
            let href = element.getAttribute("href");

            // Check to see if the the href include http:// or https://
            if (href.includes("http://") || href.includes("https://")) {
                element.target = "_blank"; // Make link open in new tab
                element.rel = "noreferrer nofollow noopener";
            }
        });
    })
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">

    setTimeout(function () {
        var x = document.getElementById("SendEmailModel_CountryCode").selectedIndex;
        var country = document.getElementsByTagName("option")[x].value;

        if (country === "USA" || country === "CAN") {
            $("div#zipCodeFeedback").removeClass("hidden");

            $("input#SendEmailModel_ZipCode").each(function () {

                $("#SendEmailModel_ZipCode").rules("add", {
                    required: true,
                    messages: {
                        required: "Please fill out this required field."
                    }
                });
            });

        } else {
            $("div#zipCodeFeedback").addClass("hidden");
            $("input#SendEmailModel_ZipCode").each(function () {
                $("#SendEmailModel_ZipCode").rules("add", {
                    required: false
                });
            });
        }
    }, 5000);

    function OnChangeCountryFeedbackEmail() {
        var x = document.getElementById("SendEmailModel_CountryCode").selectedIndex;
        var country = document.getElementsByTagName("option")[x].value;

        if (country === "USA" || country === "CAN") {
            $("input#SendEmailModel_ZipCode").each(function () {
                $("input#SendEmailModel_ZipCode").rules("add", {
                    required: true,
                    messages: {
                        required: "Please fill out this required field."
                    }
                });
            });

            $("div#zipCodeFeedback").removeClass("hidden");

        } else {

            $("input#SendEmailModel_ZipCode").each(function () {
                $(this).rules("add", {
                    required: false
                });
            });
            $("div#zipCodeFeedback").addClass("hidden");
        }
    }
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">

    setTimeout(function () {
        var x = document.getElementById("SendEmailModel_CountryCode").selectedIndex;
        var country = document.getElementsByTagName("option")[x].value;

        if (country === "USA" || country === "CAN") {
            $("div#zipCodeFeedback").removeClass("hidden");

            $("input#SendEmailModel_ZipCode").each(function () {

                $("#SendEmailModel_ZipCode").rules("add", {
                    required: true,
                    messages: {
                        required: "Please fill out this required field."
                    }
                });
            });

        } else {
            $("div#zipCodeFeedback").addClass("hidden");
            $("input#SendEmailModel_ZipCode").each(function () {
                $("#SendEmailModel_ZipCode").rules("add", {
                    required: false
                });
            });
        }
    }, 5000);

    function OnChangeCountryFeedbackEmail() {
        var x = document.getElementById("SendEmailModel_CountryCode").selectedIndex;
        var country = document.getElementsByTagName("option")[x].value;

        if (country === "USA" || country === "CAN") {
            $("input#SendEmailModel_ZipCode").each(function () {
                $("input#SendEmailModel_ZipCode").rules("add", {
                    required: true,
                    messages: {
                        required: "Please fill out this required field."
                    }
                });
            });

            $("div#zipCodeFeedback").removeClass("hidden");

        } else {

            $("input#SendEmailModel_ZipCode").each(function () {
                $(this).rules("add", {
                    required: false
                });
            });
            $("div#zipCodeFeedback").addClass("hidden");
        }
    }
</script>

<script src="/Scripts/vendor/jquery-2.2.1.min.js" type="text/javascript"></script>
<script type="text/javascript">
    $(document).ready(function () {
        var randomNumber = Math.floor(Math.random() * $("#newslettersubscribe .form-content-subscribe .row .col-sm-6").length);
        $("#newslettersubscribe .form-content-subscribe .row .col-sm-6").eq(randomNumber).append('<input type="text" class="nickNameField" value="" />')

        $(".newsletter-subscribe__modal-privacy-notice__title h5").attr('hidden', true);

        var inputData = $(".newsletter-subscribe__modal-privacy-notice__title div .subscribe-text").text();
        $(".newsletter-subscribe__modal-privacy-notice__title div p:first").append(inputData);
        $(".newsletter-subscribe__modal-privacy-notice__title div .subscribe-text").remove();

        //Set Checkbox Value 1 or 0
        $('.toi').on('change', function () {
            this.value ^= 1;
        });

        //Set AssetURL
        var assetURL = window.location.href;
        $("#newsletter_asseturl").val(assetURL);
    });

</script>

    <script>
        // Query-only check for AngularJS modules
        function moduleExists(name) {
            if (typeof angular === 'undefined') {
                return false;
            }
            try {
                angular.module(name); // query-only; throws if missing
                return true;
            } catch (e) {
                return false;
            }
        }
